# Fort Sam Houston Building 615 Controls Excerpt - ground truth

complete_with_documented_source_limitations

Source: [pdf/12__vol2__028__Fort_Sam_Houston_Building_615_Controls_Excerpt.pdf](../../pdf/12__vol2__028__Fort_Sam_Houston_Building_615_Controls_Excerpt.pdf). 9 supplied pages. SHA-256 `c62b086bc4b947bc7b87e237c415df1417ec4ebd5a3bf6b15bc7328890859d4d`.

Complete ground truth for the nine supplied pages of the Building 615 archive excerpt: project and sheet metadata, all populated schedule and BAS matrix cells, all printed written control sequences and P&ID context, logical sensor roles, and excerpt limits. This is not ground truth for the missing 182 pages of the original 191-sheet set and does not invent plan-level equipment, zone, actuator, controller, terminal, or wiring quantities.

## Reading this record

- A schedule row, a P&ID/template row, a logical sensor role and an installed physical device are different quantities. The supplied archive excerpt does not support treating them as interchangeable.
- M-601 has 32 marked equipment schedule rows. M-505 adds 16 duct-silencer-design rows and 17 external-static-pressure rows; all 65 source schedule rows and their cells are embedded in this record.
- The 193 BAS matrix rows are retained exactly in 12 P&ID/template tables. Local numeric tags are unique only within their printed table. This record does not inflate them into a building-wide terminal count.
- M-507 and M-508 are P&ID/point-list sheets without written sequences. M-509 is the printed written hydronic-sequence source; the sheets are not conflated.
- Every supplied page was visually reviewed and independently captured by MuPDF and Poppler. Parser agreement corroborates bounded text/cells, while visual review establishes page disposition and limitations; neither process invents missing project facts.

  - value: null (see recorded status/reason)

- title block scale: NOT STATED
- finding: Every supplied sheet was visually reviewed. The schedule and control schematic/detail views are diagrammatic or tabular; this excerpt does not show a printed title-block scale or a graphic scale bar for a measurable plan view. The M-505 detail captions state NOT TO SCALE where printed.
- graphic scale bar total: 0
- measurable length scaled view total: 0

### PDF 1 - M-505: HVAC SCHEDULE AND DETAILS IV

Visual review: Duct-silencer and external-static-pressure schedules; mini-split and concealed-fan-coil details. No graphic scale bar..

### PDF 2 - M-506: MECHANICAL CONTROL SCHEMATICS I

Visual review: Mini-split and exhaust-fan control schematics, meter and global I/O matrices, and written sequences..

### PDF 3 - M-507: MECHANICAL CONTROL SCHEMATICS II

Visual review: Chilled-water P&ID and BAS input/output matrix..

### PDF 4 - M-508: MECHANICAL CONTROL SCHEMATICS III

Visual review: Heating-water P&ID and BAS input/output matrix..

### PDF 5 - M-509: MECHANICAL CONTROL SCHEMATICS IV

Visual review: Written chilled-water and heating-hot-water system sequences; no separate typed point matrix..

### PDF 6 - M-510: MECHANICAL CONTROL SCHEMATICS V

Visual review: Fan-coil-unit P&ID, typed BAS input/output matrix, and written FCU sequence..

### PDF 7 - M-511: MECHANICAL CONTROL SCHEMATIC VI

Visual review: DOAS-3 and VAV-terminal P&IDs, typed BAS input/output matrices, and written sequences..

### PDF 8 - M-512: MECHANICAL CONTROL SCHEMATIC VII

Visual review: DOAS-1/2 P&ID, typed BAS input/output matrix, and written sequence..

### PDF 9 - M-601: HVAC SCHEDULES I

Visual review: FCU, DOAS, boiler, unit-heater, chiller, and exhaust-fan schedules with detailed schedule notes..

Additional printed identities / source limitations:

- Record:
  - id: M505-DUCT-SILENCER-ROWS
  - classification: Noise-control duct silencer schedule selections
  - quantity semantics: Sixteen source rows include printed quantity and location/serves values; no individual silencer tags or plan locations are supplied.
- Record:
  - id: M505-EXTERNAL-STATIC-PRESSURE-ROWS
  - classification: External-static-pressure performance-reference rows
  - quantity semantics: Seventeen source rows cover FCU and DOAS entries. Some DOAS marks repeat for separate supply/exhaust values; the schedule does not establish additional units.
- Record:
  - id: P_AND_ID_LABELS
  - classification: Schematic labels
  - quantity semantics: M-506 through M-512 labels on P&IDs/templates are not reconciled to an installed-equipment roster because the archive omits mechanical plans.

- source components:
  - points m506 airside templates:
    - document id: 12__vol2__028
    - module role: Exact transcription of the two airside BAS input/output point matrices on M-506. Numeric TAG values are local callout marks within their own P&ID; they are not globally unique BACnet point names or a count of installed controllers.
    - source sheet: M-506
    - source page: 2
    - normalization note: An X in AI, AO, DI, or DO is retained in the exact source cell. An X in ALARM is a separately printed alarm indication, not silently converted to a hardware I/O type.
    - logical row summary:
      - M506-MINI-SPLIT:
        - rows: 10
        - AI: 1

        - DO: 2
        - alarm flag: 2
      - total rows: 16
      - caution: These are printed template rows, not an installed-equipment quantity. The M-506 diagrams use TYPICAL labels and do not enumerate a complete field-device application list.

## Controls Context

This composite is an assembly of independently verified authored components. It does not create PDF facts, reconcile aliases, or infer physical quantities.

- module role: Composite authored module controls_context; each named component is embedded without rewriting its facts.
- source modules:
  - controls_context_m506
  - controls_context_m507
  - controls_context_m508
  - controls_context_m509_hydronic
  - controls_context_m510
  - controls_context_m511
  - controls_context_m512
- source components:
  - controls context m506:
    - document id: 12__vol2__028
    - module role: M-506 written-controls context: bounded source statements, network qualification, and sensor/zone limitations. It complements the exact M-506 I/O matrices; it does not create an installed-equipment or sensor count from typical diagrams.
    - source sheet: M-506
    - source page: 2
    - parser disclosures:
      - Record:
        - id: zone-temperature-degree-glyph-order
        - status: source-text-segmentation-difference
        - description: The visual source uses degree glyphs in the occupied/unoccupied temperature statements. MuPDF and Poppler both expose the wording, but Poppler places the standalone 0/F glyph text in a different native word order. The human-readable source statement is retained exactly as displayed; independent assertions deliberately stop before the affected degree-glyph runs rather than fabricating parser agreement.
    - network architecture:
      - protocols as printed:
        - BACNET
      - protocol context: The general notes say onboard BACNET controllers shall communicate with the BAS and be monitored and controlled from the GUI, with unimpeded two-way communication with all devices.
      - transport:
        - status: not_stated_in_supplied_excerpt
        - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
      - addressing baud rate controller model:
        - status: not_stated_in_supplied_excerpt
        - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
      - other named protocols:
        - status: not_present_on_M-506
        - value: []
      - assertion ids:
        - m506-bacnet-communication
        - m506-gui-alarms
    - zone temperature control:
      - presence: written sequence present on M-506
      - statements:
        - Record:
          - id: occupied
          - text: OCCUPIED MODE: THE BUILDING SHALL BE "OCCUPIED" FROM 8 A.M. (ADJ.) TO 6 P.M. (ADJ.), MONDAY THRU FRIDAY. TEMPERATURE SETPOINTS: COOLING: 78 0F (ADJ.), HEATING 68 0F (ADJ.).
          - assertion ids:
            - zone-occupied
        - Record:
          - id: unoccupied
          - text: UNOCCUPIED MODE: THE BUILDING SHALL BE "UNOCCUPIED" FROM 6 P.M. (ADJ.) TO 8 A.M. (ADJ.) MONDAY THRU FRIDAY AND ENTIRE DAY ON SATURDAY AND SUNDAY. TEMPERATURE SETPOINTS: COOLING 80 0F (ADJ.) AND HEATING: 55 0F(ADJ).
          - assertion ids:
            - zone-unoccupied
        - Record:
          - id: occupant-adjust
          - text: ZONE SETPOINT ADJUST: THE OCCUPANT SHALL BE ABLE TO ADJUST THE ZONE TEMPERATURE HEATING AND COOLING SETPOINTS AT THE ZONE SENSOR BY ±2°F.
          - assertion ids:
            - zone-adjust
        - Record:
          - id: deadband
          - text: TEMPERATURE CONTROL DEVICES SHALL BE EQUIPPED WITH DEADBANDS THAT COMPLY WITH IECC 2015.
          - assertion ids:
            - zone-deadband
      - zone mapping:
        - status: not_stated
        - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
        - note: M-506 supplies operating requirements but no plan/room-zone map or installed zone-device count.
    - sequences:
      - Record:
        - id: mini-split
        - title: MINI-SPLIT CONTROL SEQUENCE
        - equipment scope: MINI-SPLIT SYSTEM; P&ID marked as template/two generic typical AHU/CU callouts, not an installed count
        - statements:
          - Record:
            - number: 1
            - text: THE MINI-SPLIT SYSTEM SHALL REMAIN ENABLED DURING ALL HOURS.
            - assertion ids:
              - mini-enabled
          - Record:
            - number: 2
            - text: MINI-SPLIT CONTROL: THE MINI-SPLIT SYSTEM SHALL BE PROVIDED WITH A FACTORY FURNISHED ON-BOARD BACNET CONTROLLER. THE MANUFACTURER SHALL USE ITS PREFERRED SEQUENCE TO MEET THE FOLLOWING REQUIREMENTS:
            - assertion ids:
              - mini-controller
          - Record:
            - number: 2.A
            - text: THE SPACE AIR TEMPERATURE SETPOINT SHALL BE MONITORED
            - assertion ids:
              - mini-setpoint-monitor
          - Record:
            - number: 2.B
            - text: SYSTEM SHALL BE ENERGIZED TO MAINTAIN THE SETPOINTS 78°F (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - mini-maintain
          - Record:
            - number: 3
            - text: THE ONBOARD BACNET CONTROLLER SHALL COMMUNICATE WITH THE DDC TO ALLOW THE MONITORING AND CONTROL OF I/O POINTS LISTED HERE. THE SYSTEM SHALL BE ENABLED 24/7.
            - assertion ids:
              - mini-ddc
          - Record:
            - number: 4
            - text: ALARMS: THE ONBOARD BACNET CONTROLLER SHALL TRANSMIT SYSTEM ALARMS TO THE GUI. THE ONBOARD CONTROLLER SHALL, AT A MINIMUM, TRACK ALARMS LISTED IN THE I/O POINTS LIST.
            - assertion ids:
              - mini-alarms
      - Record:
        - id: switch-controlled-exhaust-fan
        - title: EXHAUST FAN CONTROL SEQUENCE
        - equipment scope: SWITCH CONTROLLED EXHAUST FAN P&ID; source does not enumerate a quantity of exhaust fans
        - statements:
          - Record:
            - number: 1
            - text: THE SYSTEM SHALL BE CONTROLLED BY THE BAS AND SHALL OPERATE AS FOLLOWS:
            - assertion ids:
              - ef-bas-control
          - Record:
            - number: 1.1
            - text: EXHAUST FANS SHALL BE ENABLED WHEN THE LIGHT SWITCH IN THE WOMEN/MEN'S RESTROOM, SHOWER/LOCKER ROOM, OR JANITOR ROOM IS ON. EXHAUST FANS SHALL ENERGIZE WHEN OCCUPANCY SENSOR IN THESE ROOMS IS TRIGGERED.
            - assertion ids:
              - ef-enable
          - Record:
            - number: 1.2
            - text: EXHAUST FANS SHALL DE-ENERGIZE WHEN NO OCCUPANCY IS DETECTED BY THE OCCUPANCY SENSORS FOR 15 MINUTES (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - ef-deenergize
          - Record:
            - number: 2
            - text: ALARMS: THE ONBOARD BACNET CONTROLLER SHALL TRANSMIT SYSTEM ALARMS TO THE GRAPHIC USER INTERFACE (GUI). THE ONBOARD CONTROLLER SHALL, AT A MINIMUM, TRACK ALARMS LISTED IN THE I/O POINTS LIST.
            - assertion ids:
              - ef-alarms

  - controls context m507:
    - document id: 12__vol2__028
    - module role: M-507 chilled-water P&ID context. This sheet contains the exact BAS I/O matrix in the companion module and a schematic/PRV design note; it does not print a written chilled-water sequence of operations.
    - source sheet: M-507
    - source page: 3
    - network architecture:
      - protocols as printed: []
      - operator interface as printed: []
      - transport:
        - status: not_stated_on_M-507
        - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
      - addressing baud rate controller model:
        - status: not_stated_on_M-507
        - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
      - limitation: M-507 identifies selected I/O as pulled from the chiller controller, but does not name a BAS protocol, controller model, transport, or addressing method. The separate M-509 written sequence is the source for BACNET requirements.
    - written sequence of operations:
      - status: not_present_on_source_sheet
      - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
      - review basis: Full-page visual review and dual-engine text capture show a chilled-water P&ID, PRV note, and BAS I/O point list rather than a written sequence-of-operations block. M-509, not this sheet, supplies the written chilled-water sequence.
    - p and id context:
      - title: CHILLED WATER SYSTEM P&ID
      - scale: NOT TO SCALE
      - system scope: The P&ID labels CH-1, CHP-1, CHP-2, and CI-1. These are schematic labels only; the supplied excerpt has no plan/equipment schedule basis for converting them to installation quantities.
      - bypass valve: 2-WAY CHILLED WATER BYPASS VALVE LOCATED AT END OF LOOP
      - controller ownership note: * INDICATES POINT PULLED FROM CHILLER CONTROLLER
      - makeup water note: The PRV note requires 4 to 5 PSI at the highest pipe elevation, same-elevation tie-in of makeup water with the expansion tank and air separator, contractor field verification of tie-in elevation, and a printed PRV-setpoint formula.
      - field connection note: The potable-water connection is field-verified and references Plumbing Sheet PP-302; it is not an asserted installed connection location.
    - sensor and zone ledger:
      - source items: []

  - controls context m508:
    - document id: 12__vol2__028
    - module role: M-508 heating-water P&ID context. This sheet contains the exact BAS I/O matrix in the companion module and a schematic/PRV design note; it does not print a written heating-water sequence of operations.
    - source sheet: M-508
    - source page: 4
    - network architecture:
      - protocols as printed: []
      - operator interface as printed: []
      - transport:
        - status: not_stated_on_M-508
        - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
      - addressing baud rate controller model:
        - status: not_stated_on_M-508
        - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
      - limitation: M-508 identifies selected I/O as pulled from the boiler controller, but does not name a BAS protocol, controller model, transport, or addressing method. The separate M-509 written sequence is the source for BACNET and HMI requirements.
    - written sequence of operations:
      - status: not_present_on_source_sheet
      - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
      - review basis: Full-page visual review and dual-engine text capture show a heating-water P&ID, PRV note, and BAS I/O point list rather than a written sequence-of-operations block. M-509, not this sheet, supplies the written heating-water sequence.
    - p and id context:
      - title: HEATING WATER SYSTEM P&ID
      - scale: NOT TO SCALE
      - system scope: The P&ID labels B-1, BCP-1, BCP-2, HWP-1, HWP-2, AS-2, and ET-2. These are schematic labels only; the supplied excerpt has no plan/equipment schedule basis for converting them to installation quantities.
      - bypass valve: 2-WAY HEATING WATER BYPASS VALVE LOCATED AT END OF LOOP
      - controller ownership note: * INDICATES POINT PULLED FROM BOILER CONTROLLER
      - makeup water note: The PRV note requires 4 to 5 PSI at the highest pipe elevation, same-elevation tie-in of makeup water with the expansion tank and air separator, contractor field verification of tie-in elevation, and a printed PRV-setpoint formula.
      - field connection note: The potable-water connection is field-verified and references Plumbing Sheet PP-302; it is not an asserted installed connection location.
      - co point scope: The I/O matrix includes a CO SENSOR row; M-509, rather than this P&ID page, provides the written mechanical-room CO safety sequence.

  - controls context m509 hydronic:
    - document id: 12__vol2__028
    - module role: M-509 written chilled-water and heating-hot-water system controls. This is a bounded source transcription of the two detailed sequences, not an inference of installed equipment quantities from schematic labels.
    - source sheet: M-509
    - source page: 5
    - network architecture:
      - protocols as printed:
        - BACNET
      - operator interface as printed:
        - HMI
        - BAS
      - protocol context: The chiller and boiler sequences call for BACNET-compatible controllers interfaced with the building BAS, with monitoring and adjustment from the HMI.
      - transport:
        - status: not_stated_in_supplied_excerpt
        - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
      - addressing baud rate controller model:
        - status: not_stated_in_supplied_excerpt
        - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
      - assertion ids:
        - chw-chiller-bacnet
        - hhw-boiler-bacnet
    - sequences:
      - Record:
        - id: chilled-water-system
        - title: CHILLED WATER SYSTEM CONTROL SEQUENCES
        - statements:
          - Record:
            - number: A
            - text: THE NEW AIR-COOLED CHILLER SHALL BE INSTALLED WITH A BACNET COMPATIBLE CONTROLLER THAT MODULATES REFRIGERANT FLOW FOR COOLING BASED ON A PRE-DEFINED CHILLED WATER SUPPLY TEMPERATURE SET POINT.
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-chiller-bacnet
          - Record:
            - number: B.a
            - text: THERE IS A CALL FOR COOLING: DEFINED AS ANY CHILLED WATER VALVE OPEN MORE THAN 20% (ADJ.) FOR A PERIOD OF 5 MINS. (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-enable-valve
          - Record:
            - number: B.b
            - text: AND OUTSIDE AIR TEMPERATURE IS GREATER THAN OR EQUAL TO 55°F (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-enable-oat
          - Record:
            - number: D
            - text: TO PREVENT SHORT CYCLING, THE CHILLED WATER SYSTEM SHALL REMAIN ENABLED OR DISABLED FOR AT LEAST 30 MINUTES (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-short-cycle
          - Record:
            - number: E
            - text: THE LEAD PUMP SHALL RUN WHEN THE CHILLER IS COMMANDED TO START. THE LEAD CHILLED WATER PUMP WILL ROTATE AFTER A RUNTIME OF 1 WEEK (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-lead-rotation
          - Record:
            - number: E.a
            - text: THE CHILLED WATER PUMP SHALL START IMMEDIATELY ON RECEIVING CHILLER START COMMAND TO PROVIDE FLOW FOR THE CHILLER.
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-pump-start
          - Record:
            - number: E.b
            - text: THE CHILLED WATER PUMP SHALL DISABLE AFTER 15 MINUTES (ADJ.) OF RECEIVING THE CHILLER STOP COMMAND TO MAINTAIN FLOW UNTIL THE CHILLER SHUTS DOWN.
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-pump-stop-delay
          - Record:
            - number: F.b
            - text: THE BAS SHALL BE ABLE TO HAVE TWO-WAY COMMUNICATION WITH THE CHILLER CONTROLLER AND MONITOR AND ADJUST CHILLED WATER SET POINT BASED ON THE RESET STRATEGY.
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-two-way
          - Record:
            - number: G
            - text: THE CONTROLLER SHALL MEASURE CHILLED WATER DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE WITH A SEPARATE DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE SENSOR IN THE LOOP AND MODULATE THE CHILLED WATER PUMP ECM TO MAINTAIN ITS CHILLED WATER DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE SET POINT (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-dp-reset
          - Record:
            - number: G.a
            - text: THE ECM MINIMUM SPEED SHALL NOT DROP BELOW THE SPEED CORRESPONDING WITH 5% ABOVE THE CHILLER MANUFACTURER’S RECOMMENDED MINIMUM CHILLED WATER FLOW.
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-min-speed
          - Record:
            - number: G.b
            - text: BAS SHALL MONITOR ALL OF THE CHILLED WATER CONTROL VALVES EVERY (5) FIVE MINUTES (ADJ.) AND USE THE MOST WIDE-OPEN COIL POSITION IN THE ENTIRE SYSTEM AS THE CRITICAL VALVE.
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-critical-valve
          - Record:
            - number: G.c
            - text: IF THE CRITICAL CHILLED WATER CONTROL VALVE IS MORE THAN 90% OPEN (ADJ.), THEN INCREASE THE DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE SET POINT BY 1 PSI (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-dp-increase
          - Record:
            - number: G.f
            - text: IN THE EVENT THAT THE CRITICAL CHILLED WATER CONTROL VALVE IS LESS THAN 80% OPEN (ADJ.), AND THE DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE SET POINT HAS BEEN REDUCED TO THE MINIMUM ALLOWABLE SYSTEM LIMIT, THE CHILLED WATER BYPASS VALVE LOCATED AT THE END OF LOOP SHALL BE OPENED TO ENSURE ADEQUATE CHILLED FLOW IS DELIVERED TO THE CHILLER.
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-bypass
          - Record:
            - number: H.g
            - text: FREEZE PROTECTION: IF THERE IS NO CALL FOR COOLING, OUTSIDE AIR TEMPERATURE IS BELOW 38°F. PROVIDE ALARM AND START CHILLED WATER PUMPS TO SEND WATER IS FLOWING THRU THE CHILLER.
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-freeze
          - Record:
            - number: H.h
            - text: CHILLER SHUTDOWN SEQUENCE: THE CHILLER WILL GO THROUGH A SHUTDOWN SEQUENCE IF CHILLER START CONDITIONS ARE NOT MET. THE CHILLER WILL DISABLE FIRST AFTER GOING THROUGH ITS INTERNAL SHUTDOWN PROCEDURE AND THE LEAD CHILLED WATER PUMP SHALL DISABLE AFTER A DELAY OF 15 MINS. (ADJ.)
            - assertion ids:
              - chw-shutdown
      - Record:
        - id: heating-hot-water-system
        - title: HEATING-WATER SYSTEM CONTROL SEQUENCES
        - statements:
          - Record:
            - number: A
            - text: THE HEATING WATER SYSTEM SHALL BE ENERGIZED AND CONTROLLED BY THE BUILDING BAS SYSTEM TO MAINTAIN A PRE-DEFINED HEATING WATER TEMPERATURE SETPOINT. ALL CONTROL POINTS ON THE I/O POINTS LIST SHALL BE MONITORED AND/OR CONTROLLED VIA THE BAS SYSTEM THROUGH THE HMI.
            - assertion ids:
              - hhw-bas-hmi
          - Record:
            - number: B.a
            - text: THERE IS A CALL FOR HEATING: DEFINED AS ANY HEATING WATER CONTROL VALVE OPEN MORE THAN 20% (ADJ.) FOR A PERIOD OF 5 MINS. (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - hhw-enable-valve
          - Record:
            - number: B.b
            - text: OUTSIDE AIR TEMPERATURE LESS THAN 65°F (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - hhw-enable-oat
          - Record:
            - number: C
            - text: THE HEATING WATER SYSTEM ENABLE POINT SHALL HAVE THE CAPABILITY OF BEING COMMANDED AND/OR OVERRIDDEN FROM THE HMI BY THE USER. TO PREVENT SHORT CYCLING, THE BOILER SYSTEM SHALL REMAIN ENABLED OR DISABLED FOR 30 MINS.(ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - hhw-override-short-cycle
          - Record:
            - number: D
            - text: THE TWO HEATING WATER SYSTEM PUMPS SHALL OPERATE IN A LEAD/STANDBY FASHION, WITH ONLY ONE PUMP RUNNING AT A TIME. THE SYSTEM PUMP SHALL RUN ANY TIME A HEATING WATER CONTROL VALVE IS OPEN MORE THAN 20% (ADJ.) FOR A PERIOD OF 5 MINS. (ADJ.). THE MOST OPEN VALVE IN THE SYSTEM IS HENCEFORTH TERMED THE “CRITICAL CONTROL VALVE”.
            - assertion ids:
              - hhw-lead-standby
          - Record:
            - number: D.b
            - text: ON FAILURE OF THE LEAD PUMP, THE STANDBY PUMP SHALL RUN AND THE LEAD PUMP SHALL TURN OFF. CONTROL SYSTEM SHALL DISPLAY A PUMP FAILURE ALARM FOR THE LEAD PUMP ON THE HMI.
            - assertion ids:
              - hhw-standby-failure
          - Record:
            - number: D.f
            - text: FREEZE PROTECTION: THE HEATING WATER SYSTEM PUMPS SHALL RUN FOR FREEZE PROTECTION WHENEVER OUTSIDE AIR TEMPERATURE IS LESS THAN 38°F (ADJUSTABLE). THE SYSTEM BYPASS CONTROL VALVE SHALL MODULATE OPEN AS REQUIRED TO ALLOW BOTH SYSTEM PUMPS TO RUN (AT 40% SPEED RESPECTIVELY) AS REQUIRED FOR CIRCULATION. THE CONTROL SYSTEM SHALL DISPLAY FREEZE ALARM ON THE HMI FOR THE TIME THIS SEQUENCE IS ON.
            - assertion ids:
              - hhw-freeze
          - Record:
            - number: E
            - text: THE CONTROLLER SHALL MEASURE HEATING WATER DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE WITH A SEPARATE DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE SENSOR IN THE LOOP AND MODULATE THE HEATING WATER PUMP ECM TO MAINTAIN ITS HEATING WATER DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE SET POINT.
            - assertion ids:
              - hhw-dp-reset
          - Record:
            - number: E.a
            - text: THE ECM MINIMUM SPEED SHALL NOT DROP BELOW THE SPEED CORRESPONDING WITH 30% OF THE MAXIMUM HEATING WATER FLOW.
            - assertion ids:
              - hhw-min-speed
          - Record:
            - number: E.c
            - text: IF THE CRITICAL HEATING CONTROL VALVE IS MORE THAN 90% OPEN (ADJ.), THEN INCREASE THE DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE SET POINT BY 1 PSI (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - hhw-dp-increase
          - Record:
            - number: E.f
            - text: IN THE EVENT THAT THE CRITICAL HEATING WATER CONTROL VALVE IS LESS THAN 80% OPEN (ADJ.), AND THE DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE SET POINT HAS BEEN REDUCED TO THE MINIMUM ALLOWABLE SYSTEM LIMIT, THE HEATING WATER SYSTEM BYPASS VALVE LOCATED AT THE END OF LOOP SHALL BE OPENED TO ENSURE ADEQUATE HEATING FLOW IS DELIVERED TO PROTECT THE PUMP MOTORS.
            - assertion ids:
              - hhw-bypass
          - Record:
            - number: G
            - text: THE BOILER CIRCULATOR PUMPS SHALL BE CONSTANT SPEED AND CONTROLLED IN A LEAD/LAG FASHION. THE LEAD PUMP SHALL ROTATE WHEN ITS RUNTIME EXCEEDS ONE WEEK (ADJ.) OR WHEN THE LEAD PUMP FAILS. WHEN THERE IS A CALL FOR HEATING THE LEAD PUMP SHALL BE ENERGIZED.
            - assertion ids:
              - boiler-circulator
          - Record:
            - number: H
            - text: THE BOILER SHALL BE PROVIDED WITH A BACNET COMPATIBLE STANDALONE BOILER SYSTEM CONTROLLER. THE CONTROLLER SHALL CONTROL ALL OPERATIONS AND ENERGY INPUT (MODULATE COMBUSTION) TO MAINTAIN THE HOT WATER SUPPLY TEMPERATURE SETPOINT. THE BOILER SYSTEM CONTROLLER SHALL BE INTERFACED TO THE BAS AND ALLOW MONITORING AND ADJUSTMENT OF SET POINTS.
            - assertion ids:
              - hhw-boiler-bacnet
          - Record:
            - number: H.c
            - text: WHENEVER THE HEATING WATER SYSTEM IS IN FREEZE PROTECTION MODE DUE TO LOW OUTSIDE AIR TEMPERATURE, WATER SHALL FLOW THROUGH THE BOILER CONTINUOUSLY. IF THERE IS NO CALL FOR HEATING, BUT HEATING WATER TEMPERATURE DROPS BELOW 35°F (ADJ.), BOILER SHALL START AND INCREASE WATER TEMPERATURE TO 45°F (ADJ.)
            - assertion ids:
              - boiler-freeze
          - Record:
            - number: I.g
            - text: CO SENSOR: THE CO SENSOR SHALL CONTINUOUSLY MONITOR CARBON MONOXIDE CONTENT INSIDE THE MECHANICAL ROOM AND SHALL BE INTERLOCKED WITH THE BAS SYSTEM AND IF A CO LEVEL OF MORE THAN 100 PPM IS DETECTED, IT SHALL ALARM THE BAS SYSTEM TO SHUTDOWN THE BURNER IN THE BOILER AND STOP COMBUSTION PROCESS IMMEDIATELY.
            - assertion ids:
              - boiler-co-safety
    - sensor and zone ledger:
      - source items:
        - Record:
          - id: CHW-DP
          - classification: Separate chilled-water-loop differential-pressure sensor
          - physical sensor count:
            - status: not_stated

          - basis: M-509 chilled-water sequence G.
        - Record:

        - Record:

          - physical sensor count:

        - Record:

          - physical sensor count:

      - counts:

          - status: not_stated
          - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
        - CO sensors:
          - status: not_stated

        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: hhw-enable-valve
        - page: 5
        - bbox:
          - 1070
          - 165
          - 2200
          - 185
        - expected: THERE IS A CALL FOR HEATING: DEFINED AS ANY HEATING WATER CONTROL VALVE OPEN MORE THAN 20% (ADJ.) FOR A PERIOD OF 5 MINS. (ADJ.).
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: hhw-enable-oat
        - page: 5
        - bbox:
          - 1070
          - 185
          - 1850
          - 202
        - expected: OUTSIDE AIR TEMPERATURE LESS THAN 65°F (ADJ.).
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: hhw-override-short-cycle
        - page: 5
        - bbox:
          - 1070
          - 200
          - 2200
          - 235
        - expected: THE HEATING WATER SYSTEM ENABLE POINT SHALL HAVE THE CAPABILITY OF BEING COMMANDED AND/OR OVERRIDDEN FROM THE HMI BY THE USER. TO PREVENT SHORT CYCLING, THE BOILER SYSTEM SHALL REMAIN ENABLED OR DISABLED FOR 30 MINS.(ADJ.).
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: hhw-lead-standby
        - page: 5
        - bbox:
          - 1070
          - 235
          - 2200
          - 260
        - expected: THE TWO HEATING WATER SYSTEM PUMPS SHALL OPERATE
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: hhw-standby-failure
        - page: 5
        - bbox:
          - 1070
          - 302
          - 2200
          - 320
        - expected: ON FAILURE OF THE LEAD PUMP, THE STANDBY PUMP SHALL RUN AND THE LEAD PUMP SHALL TURN OFF. CONTROL SYSTEM SHALL DISPLAY A PUMP FAILURE ALARM FOR THE LEAD PUMP
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: hhw-freeze
        - page: 5
        - bbox:
          - 1070
          - 390
          - 2200
          - 440
        - expected: FREEZE PROTECTION: THE HEATING WATER SYSTEM PUMPS SHALL RUN FOR FREEZE PROTECTION WHENEVER OUTSIDE AIR TEMPERATURE IS LESS THAN 38°F (ADJUSTABLE). THE SYSTEM BYPASS CONTROL VALVE SHALL MODULATE OPEN AS REQUIRED TO ALLOW BOTH SYSTEM PUMPS TO RUN (AT 40% SPEED RESPECTIVELY) AS REQUIRED FOR CIRCULATION. THE CONTROL SYSTEM SHALL DISPLAY FREEZE ALARM ON THE HMI FOR THE TIME THIS SEQUENCE IS ON.
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: hhw-dp-reset
        - page: 5
        - bbox:
          - 1070
          - 456
          - 2200
          - 525
        - expected: THE CONTROLLER SHALL MEASURE HEATING WATER DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE WITH A SEPARATE DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE SENSOR IN THE LOOP AND MODULATE THE HEATING WATER PUMP ECM TO MAINTAIN ITS HEATING WATER DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE SET POINT.
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: hhw-min-speed
        - page: 5
        - bbox:
          - 1070
          - 525
          - 2150
          - 555
        - expected: THE ECM MINIMUM SPEED SHALL NOT DROP BELOW THE SPEED CORRESPONDING WITH 30% OF THE MAXIMUM HEATING WATER FLOW.
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: hhw-dp-increase
        - page: 5
        - bbox:
          - 1070
          - 592
          - 2150
          - 610
        - expected: IF THE CRITICAL HEATING CONTROL VALVE IS MORE THAN 90% OPEN (ADJ.), THEN INCREASE THE DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE SET POINT BY 1 PSI (ADJ.).
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: hhw-bypass
        - page: 5
        - bbox:
          - 1070
          - 680
          - 2200
          - 700
        - expected: IN THE EVENT THAT THE CRITICAL HEATING WATER CONTROL VALVE IS LESS THAN 80% OPEN (ADJ.), AND THE DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE SET POINT HAS BEEN REDUCED TO THE MINIMUM
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: boiler-circulator
        - page: 5
        - bbox:
          - 1070
          - 815
          - 2200
          - 855
        - expected: THE BOILER CIRCULATOR PUMPS SHALL BE CONSTANT SPEED AND CONTROLLED IN A LEAD/LAG FASHION. THE LEAD PUMP SHALL ROTATE WHEN ITS RUNTIME EXCEEDS ONE WEEK (ADJ.) OR WHEN THE LEAD PUMP FAILS. WHEN THERE IS A CALL FOR HEATING THE LEAD PUMP SHALL BE ENERGIZED.
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: hhw-boiler-bacnet
        - page: 5
        - bbox:
          - 1070
          - 970
          - 2200
          - 1020
        - expected: THE BOILER SHALL BE PROVIDED WITH A BACNET COMPATIBLE STANDALONE BOILER SYSTEM CONTROLLER. THE CONTROLLER SHALL CONTROL ALL OPERATIONS AND ENERGY INPUT (MODULATE COMBUSTION) TO MAINTAIN THE HOT WATER SUPPLY TEMPERATURE SETPOINT. THE BOILER SYSTEM CONTROLLER SHALL BE INTERFACED TO THE BAS AND ALLOW MONITORING AND ADJUSTMENT OF SET POINTS.
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: boiler-freeze
        - page: 5
        - bbox:
          - 1070
          - 1120
          - 2200
          - 1155
        - expected: WHENEVER THE HEATING WATER SYSTEM IS IN FREEZE PROTECTION MODE DUE TO LOW OUTSIDE AIR TEMPERATURE, WATER SHALL FLOW THROUGH THE BOILER CONTINUOUSLY. IF THERE IS NO CALL FOR HEATING, BUT HEATING WATER TEMPERATURE DROPS BELOW 35°F (ADJ.), BOILER SHALL START AND INCREASE WATER TEMPERATURE TO 45°F (ADJ.)
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: boiler-co-safety
        - page: 5
        - bbox:
          - 1070
          - 1325
          - 2200
          - 1345
        - expected: CO SENSOR: THE CO SENSOR SHALL CONTINUOUSLY MONITOR CARBON MONOXIDE CONTENT INSIDE THE MECHANICAL ROOM AND SHALL BE INTERLOCKED WITH THE BAS SYSTEM
        - mode: contains

  - controls context m510:
    - document id: 12__vol2__028
    - module role: M-510 FCU written-controls context. It complements the exact FCU P&ID matrix and does not convert this reusable sequence into an installed FCU or sensor count.
    - source sheet: M-510
    - source page: 6
    - sequences:
      - Record:
        - id: fcu
        - title: FAN COIL UNIT (FCU) CONTROL SEQUENCE
        - equipment scope: FCU P&ID/sequence template; installed FCU quantity and zone mapping are not stated on this source sheet.
        - statements:
          - Record:
            - number: A
            - text: THE FAN COIL UNIT WILL CONSIST OF CHILLED WATER COOLING COIL, HOT WATER HEATING COIL, INTEGRAL VARIABLE SPEED DIRECT DRIVE SUPPLY AIR FAN, RETURN AIR DAMPER, AND FILTER.
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-components
          - Record:
            - number: B.a-d
            - text: Start/stop bases: preset time-of-day occupancy schedule through the BAS; unoccupied setback/set-up; local zone-thermostat override; or manual/remote BAS override.
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-startstop-title
              - fcu-startstop-schedule
              - fcu-startstop-setback
              - fcu-startstop-local
              - fcu-startstop-bas
          - Record:
            - number: C
            - text: WHEN THE FAN COIL UNIT IS OFF, THE CHILLED WATER CONTROL VALVE SHALL BE FULLY CLOSED TO FLOW THROUGH THE COIL.
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-off-chw
          - Record:
            - number: D
            - text: WHEN THE FAN COIL UNIT IS ENERGIZED BY OCCUPANCY SCHEDULE, THE SUPPLY AIR FAN SHALL RUN CONTINUOUSLY. THE FAN SPEED SHALL MODULATE ACCORDING TO THE MANUFACTURER'S PREFERRED SEQUENCE TO MAINTAIN A SPACE TEMPERATURE OF 78°F (ADJ.) IN COOLING AND 68°F (ADJ.) IN HEATING.
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-fan-control
          - Record:
            - number: E.a
            - text: COOLING: UNDER NORMAL OPERATING CONDITIONS THE AVERAGING TYPE SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE SENSOR (LOCATED IN UNIT DISCHARGE) WILL, ACTING THROUGH THE CONTROLLER, MODULATE THE CHILLED WATER COIL TO MAINTAIN A SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE OF 55°F (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-cooling-sat
          - Record:
            - number: E.b
            - text: HEATING: UNDER NORMAL OPERATING CONDITIONS THE AVERAGING TYPE SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE SENSOR (LOCATED IN UNIT DISCHARGE) WILL, ACTING THROUGH THE CONTROLLER, MODULATE THE HOT WATER COIL TO MAINTAIN A SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE OF 85°F (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-heating-sat
          - Record:
            - number: F
            - text: During unoccupied cooling, BAS energizes the chilled-water coil above 85°F (ADJ.) and operates it until space temperature drops to 83°F (ADJ.); the return-air damper remains fully open.
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-unoccupied
              - fcu-ra-damper
          - Record:
            - number: G
            - text: An averaging-type manual-reset freezestat in the mixed-air section fully opens the chilled-water control valve at 38°F (ADJ.), alarms BAS, and requires manual reset before return to normal operation.
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-freeze
          - Record:
            - number: H
            - text: Emergency shutdown is hard wired into the supply-fan motor-control circuit. It shuts down the fan, alarms BAS, and fully closes all control dampers and hydronic control valves.
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-esd
          - Record:
            - number: I
            - text: Only one coil—heating or cooling—may operate at a time, except in freeze-protection mode.
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-one-coil
          - Record:
            - number: J.a
            - text: Supply-fan failure uses a current transformer: commanded on with no detected current alarms BAS.
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-fan-failure
          - Record:
            - number: J.c
            - text: High/low supply-air-temperature alarm monitors the averaging sensor in the unit discharge; thresholds are above 60°F (ADJ.) or below 45°F (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-sat-alarm
          - Record:
            - number: J.d-e
            - text: High and low supply-static-pressure switches in the unit discharge are hard wired to shut down the supply fan and alarm BAS at above 4 inches W.C. (ADJ.) or below 0.5 inches W.C. (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-static-alarms
          - Record:
            - number: J.f
            - text: A return-air-duct smoke detector is hard wired to shut down the fan, with auxiliary notification to fire alarm and BAS.
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-smoke
          - Record:
            - number: J.g-h
            - text: A differential-pressure switch across the filter alarms at a 0.70-inch static-pressure high limit (ADJ.); condensate float-switch overflow is hard wired to shut down the fan and alarm BAS.
            - assertion ids:
              - fcu-filter
              - fcu-condensate
    - sensor and zone ledger:
      - source items:
        - Record:
          - id: FCU-SAT

        - Record:

          - physical sensor count:

          - zone: FCU discharge
          - basis: M-510 J.d/J.e.
        - Record:

          - status: not_stated

        - CO2 sensors:
          - status: not_stated
          - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
        - smart thermostats:
          - status: not_stated
          - value: null (see recorded status/reason)

        - mode: contains

        - expected: START/STOP CONTROL: THE FAN COIL UNIT SHALL START AND STOP BASED ON THE FOLLOWING:
        - mode: contains

        - page: 6

        - mode: contains

        - page: 6
        - bbox:

        - mode: contains
      - Record:

        - expected: OR LOCAL OVERRIDE OF ZONE THERMOSTAT.
        - mode: contains

        - expected: OR MANUAL/REMOTE OVERRIDE OF SYSTEM THROUGH THE BAS.
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: fcu-off-chw
        - page: 6
        - bbox:
          - 120
          - 778
          - 2200
          - 802
        - expected: WHEN THE FAN COIL UNIT IS OFF, THE CHILLED WATER CONTROL VALVE SHALL BE FULLY CLOSED TO FLOW THROUGH THE COIL.
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: fcu-fan-control
        - page: 6
        - bbox:
          - 120
          - 812
          - 2200
          - 850
        - expected: SUPPLY AIR FAN CONTROL: WHEN THE FAN COIL UNIT IS ENERGIZED BY OCCUPANCY SCHEDULE, THE SUPPLY AIR FAN SHALL RUN CONTINUOUSLY. THE FAN SPEED SHALL MODULATE ACCORDING TO THE MANUFACTURER'S PREFERRED SEQUENCE TO MAINTAIN A SPACE TEMPERATURE OF 78°F (ADJ.) IN COOLING AND 68°F (ADJ.) IN HEATING.
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: fcu-cooling-sat
        - page: 6
        - bbox:
          - 150
          - 880
          - 2200
          - 913
        - expected: COOLING: UNDER NORMAL OPERATING CONDITIONS THE AVERAGING TYPE SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE SENSOR (LOCATED IN UNIT DISCHARGE) WILL, ACTING THROUGH THE CONTROLLER, MODULATE THE CHILLED WATER COIL TO MAINTAIN A SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE OF 55°F (ADJ.).
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: fcu-heating-sat
        - page: 6
        - bbox:
          - 150
          - 914
          - 2200
          - 947
        - expected: HEATING: UNDER NORMAL OPERATING CONDITIONS THE AVERAGING TYPE SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE SENSOR (LOCATED IN UNIT DISCHARGE) WILL, ACTING THROUGH THE CONTROLLER, MODULATE THE HOT WATER COIL TO MAINTAIN A SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE OF 85°F (ADJ.).
        - mode: contains

        - page: 6
        - bbox:

          - 966
          - 2200
          - 1002
        - expected: UNOCCUPIED SETUP CONTROL: THE FAN COIL UNIT SHALL BE PROVIDED WITH UNOCCUPIED SETUP AND UNOCCUPIED SETBACK TEMPERATURE CONTROLS. DURING UNOCCUPIED CONDITIONS, THE BAS SHALL ENERGIZE THE CHILLED WATER COIL IF ANY ZONE’S SPACE AIR TEMPERATURE RISES ABOVE 85°F (ADJ.) DURING UNOCCUPIED HOURS AND THE ZONE IS IN COOLING MODE. ONCE ENERGIZED, THE CHILLED WATER COIL SHALL OPERATE UNDER NORMAL CONDITIONS UNTIL SPACE TEMPERATURE DROPS TO 83°F (ADJ.) FOR UNOCCUPIED SETUP.
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: fcu-ra-damper
        - page: 6
        - bbox:
          - 120
          - 1000
          - 1600
          - 1018
        - expected: RETURN AIR DAMPER SHALL REMAIN FULLY OPEN TO ALLOW FLOW THROUGH THE FAN COIL UNIT.

        - bbox:

          - 1034
          - 2200
          - 1070
        - expected: FREEZE PROTECTION: AN AVERAGING TYPE MANUAL-RESET FREEZESTAT INSTALLED IN THE MIXED AIR SECTION OF THE FAN COIL UNIT SHALL FULLY OPEN THE CHILLED WATER CONTROL VALVE TO ALLOW FLOW THROUGH THE COIL SHOULD THE MIXED AIR TEMPERATURE DROP TO 38°F (ADJ.). THE FREEZESTAT SHALL SEND AN ALARM TO THE BAS. A MANUAL RESET OF THE FREEZESTAT SHALL BE REQUIRED FOR THE FAN COIL UNIT TO RETURN TO NORMAL OPERATING CONDITION.

        - id: fcu-esd
        - page: 6
        - bbox:

          - 1085
          - 2200
          - 1120
        - expected: EMERGENCY SHUTDOWN: AN EMERGENCY SHUTDOWN SWITCH SHALL BE HARD WIRED IN TO SUPPLY FAN MOTOR CONTROL CIRCUIT TO SHUT DOWN THE FAN AND SEND AN ALARM TO THE BAS WHEN THE EMERGENCY SHUTDOWN SWITCH IS ACTIVATED. ALL CONTROL DAMPERS AND HYDRONIC CONTROL VALVES, REGARDLESS OF WHAT MODE THE UNIT IS IN, SHALL FULLY CLOSE WHEN EMERGENCY SHUTDOWN HAS BEEN ACTIVATED.
        - mode: contains

        - page: 6
        - bbox:
          - 120
          - 1136
          - 2200
          - 1160
        - expected: THE BAS SHALL ALLOW OPERATION OF ONLY ONE COIL (EITHER HEATING OR COOLING) AT A TIME. UNDER NO CONDITION SHALL BOTH HEATING AND COOLING VALVES BE SIMULTANEOUSLY OPEN OTHER THAN WHEN THE UNIT IS IN FREEZE PROTECTION MODE.
        - mode: contains

        - bbox:
          - 120
          - 1187
          - 2200
          - 1205
        - expected: SUPPLY FAN FAILURE: A CURRENT TRANSFORMER (CT) INSTALLED ON THE SUPPLY FAN CIRCUIT SHALL SEND AN ALARM TO THE BAS IF THE FAN IS COMMANDED ON AND NO CURRENT IS DETECTED.
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: fcu-sat-alarm
        - page: 6
        - bbox:
          - 120
          - 1221
          - 2200
          - 1239
        - expected: HIGH AND LOW SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE: THE BAS SHALL MONITOR THE AVERAGING TYPE SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE INSTALLED IN THE UNIT DISCHARGE AND SENDS AN ALARM IF THE SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE EXCEEDS 60°F (ADJ.) OR DROPS BELOW 45°F (ADJ.).
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: fcu-static-alarms
        - page: 6
        - bbox:
          - 120
          - 1239
          - 2200
          - 1308
        - expected: HIGH SUPPLY STATIC PRESSURE ALARM: AN ADJUSTABLE TYPE HIGH STATIC PRESSURE SWITCH, INSTALLED IN THE UNIT DISCHARGE SHALL BE HARD WIRED INTO SUPPLY FAN MOTOR CONTROL CIRCUIT TO SHUT DOWN THE SUPPLY FAN AND SEND AN ALARM TO THE BAS WHEN THE SUPPLY AIR STATIC PRESSURE EXCEEDS 4” W.C. (ADJ.).
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: fcu-smoke
        - page: 6
        - bbox:
          - 120
          - 1307
          - 2200
          - 1325
        - expected: SMOKE DETECTOR ALARM: SMOKE DETECTOR LOCATED IN THE RETURN AIR DUCTWORK SHALL BE HARD WIRED INTO THE MOTOR CONTROL CIRCUIT TO SHUT DOWN THE FAN. AN AUXILIARY CONTACT WILL NOTIFY THE FIRE ALARM SYSTEM AND SEND AN ALARM TO THE BAS.
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: fcu-filter
        - page: 6
        - bbox:
          - 120
          - 1325
          - 2200
          - 1342
        - expected: CLOGGED FILTER ALARM: A DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE SWITCH INSTALLED ACROSS THE FILTER SECTION WILL, THROUGH THE CONTROLLER, ACTIVATE AN ALARM WHEN THE FILTER PRESSURE LOSS EXCEEDS THE HIGH LIMIT SETPOINT OF 0.70 INCH STATIC PRESSURE (ADJ.).
        - mode: contains

        - id: fcu-condensate
        - page: 6
        - bbox:

          - 1342

          - 1362
        - expected: CONDENSATE OVERFLOW ALARM: A CONDENSATE FLOAT SWITCH INSTALLED IN THE AHU CONDENSATE DRAIN SHALL BE HARD WIRED INTO THE MOTOR CONTROL CIRCUIT TO SHUT DOWN THE FAN. AN AUXILIARY CONTACT WILL NOTIFY AND SEND AN ALARM TO THE BAS.

  - controls context m511:
    - document id: 12__vol2__028
    - module role: M-511 written DOAS-3 and non-fan-powered VAV controls context. This module retains source-bounded sequences, protocol context, and logical sensor roles without converting reusable P&ID rows into installed-device counts.
    - source sheet: M-511
    - source page: 7
    - network architecture:
      - protocols as printed:
        - BACNET
      - protocol context: The source requires a factory-furnished onboard BACNET controller for DOAS control. It must communicate with the BAS; the BAS enables the system and all listed I/O is to be monitored and controlled from the GUI.
      - transport:
        - status: not_stated_in_supplied_excerpt
        - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
      - addressing baud rate controller model:
        - status: not_stated_in_supplied_excerpt
        - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
      - assertion ids:
        - doas-factory-controller
        - doas-bas-gui
    - sequences:
      - Record:
        - id: doas3
        - title: DEDICATED OUTSIDE AIR SYSTEM CONTROL SEQUENCE
        - equipment scope: DOAS 3 P&ID template; the source does not state an installed DOAS-3 controller count or address.
        - statements:
          - Record:
            - number: 1.1
            - text: THE DOAS SHALL BE A PACKAGED UNIT WITH A SUPPLY FAN AND EXHAUST FAN.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas-packaged-fans
          - Record:
            - number: 1.2
            - text: THE DOAS SHALL BE PROVIDED WITH A VFD FOR THE SUPPLY FAN, AND A VFD FOR THE EXHAUST FAN.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas-vfds
          - Record:
            - number: 1.3
            - text: THE DOAS SHALL BE CONTROLLED BY THE BAS AND REMAIN ENABLED AT ALL TIMES.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas-bas-enabled
          - Record:
            - number: 1.3.1
            - text: DURING UNOCCUPIED HOURS: THE DOAS SHALL RUN AT 30% OF ITS FULL LOAD AND PROVIDE A MINIMUM AMOUNT OF OA AT ALL TIMES.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas-unoccupied
          - Record:
            - number: 1.3.2
            - text: DURING OCCUPIED HOURS: WHEN OCCUPANCY IS DETECTED FOR MORE THAN 5 MINUTES, THE DOAS SHALL RUN AT A FULL LOAD CONTINUOUSLY.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas-occupied
          - Record:
            - number: 1.3.3
            - text: SUPPLY AND EXHAUST AIR VOLUME SHALL BE BASED ON THE FEEDBACK FROM AIR FLOW MEASURE STATIONS.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas-airflow-feedback
          - Record:
            - number: 2
            - text: DOAS CONTROL: THE DOAS SYSTEM SHALL BE PROVIDED WITH A FACTORY FURNISHED ON-BOARD BACNET CONTROLLER. THE MANUFACTURER SHALL USE ITS PREFERRED SEQUENCE TO MEET THE FOLLOWING REQUIREMENTS:
            - assertion ids:
              - doas-factory-controller
          - Record:
            - number: 2.1
            - text: IN COOLING, THE CONTROLLER SHALL MONITOR THE CHILLED WATER COIL LEAVING AIR TEMPERATURE AND MODULATE CHILLED WATER FLOW TO MAINTAIN THE CHILLED WATER COIL LEAVING AIR TEMPERATURE SET POINT OF 55°F.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas-cooling
          - Record:
            - number: 2.2
            - text: IN HEATING, THE CONTROLLER SHALL MONITOR THE SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE AND MODULATE THE HOT WATER COIL TO MAINTAIN THE SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE SETPOINT OF 70°F.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas-heating
          - Record:
            - number: 2.3
            - text: IN ECONOMIZER, THE UNIT SHALL RUN USING AN OUTSIDE AIR DRY-BULB BASED CONTROL STRATEGY.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas-economizer
          - Record:
            - number: 3.1
            - text: SUPPLY AIR FAN: THE CONTROLLER SHALL MONITOR DUCT STATIC PRESSURE AND MODULATE SUPPLY AIR FLOW THROUGH THE VFD TO MAINTAIN DOWNSTREAM STATIC PRESSURE SET POINT. SENSOR LOCATION AND SETPOINT SHALL BE DETERMINED BY THE TAB FIRM.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas-supply-fan-static
          - Record:
            - number: 3.2
            - text: EXHAUST AIR FAN: EXHAUST AIR SHALL MODULATE TO BE 200 CFM LESS THAN SUPPLY AIR TO MAINTAIN A POSITIVE STATIC PRESSURE FOR THE SPACE.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas-exhaust-offset
          - Record:
            - number: 4.1
            - text: THE ONBOARD BACNET CONTROLLER SHALL COMMUNICATE WITH THE BAS TO ALLOW AT THE MINIMUM MONITORING AND CONTROL OF I/O POINTS LISTED HERE. THE SYSTEM SHALL BE ENABLED BY THE BAS. ALL CONTROL POINTS ON THE I/O POINTS LIST SHALL BE MONITORED AND CONTROLLED FROM THE GRAPHIC USER INTERFACE (GUI).
            - assertion ids:
              - doas-bas-gui
          - Record:
            - number: 5.1
            - text: THE ONBOARD BACNET CONTROLLER SHALL TRANSMIT SYSTEM ALARMS TO THE GUI. THE ONBOARD CONTROLLER SHALL, AT A MINIMUM, TRACK ALARMS LISTED IN THE I/O POINTS LIST.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas-alarms
      - Record:
        - id: vav-terminal
        - title: SEQUENCE OF OPERATION VAV TERMINAL UNIT (NON-FAN POWERED)
        - equipment scope: VAV-terminal P&ID template; no installed VAV quantity, controller address, or room-zone mapping is printed on this sheet.
        - statements:
          - Record:
            - number: 1
            - text: EACH VAV TERMINAL UNIT WILL BE PROVIDED WITH A BACNET COMPATIBLE TERMINAL EQUIPMENT CONTROLLER (TEC), A DUAL TECHNOLOGY OCCUPANCY SENSOR, A DOWNSTREAM STATIC PRESSURE SENSOR, AND A PRIMARY MODULATING SUPPLY AIR DAMPER.
            - assertion ids:
              - vav-components
          - Record:
            - number: 2
            - text: THE VAV SYSTEM SHALL BE CONTROLLED BY THE BAS AS FOLLOWS:
            - assertion ids:
              - vav-bas-control
          - Record:
            - number: 2.1
            - text: EACH VAV SHALL MONITOR ITS ASSOCIATED OCCUPANCY SENSOR IN THEIR RESPECTIVE AREA. THE GUI SHALL DISPLAY THE STATUS OF THE OCCUPANCY SENSOR IN THE BOX'S RESPECTIVE ZONE.
            - assertion ids:
              - vav-occupancy-gui
          - Record:
            - number: 2.2
            - text: SUPPLY AIR DAMPER WILL MODULATE TO BE AT AN OPEN POSITION FOR 30% OF TOTAL OUTSIDE AIR VOLUME (CALCULATED PER ASHRAE 62.1) AT ALL TIMES.
            - assertion ids:
              - vav-minimum-oa
          - Record:
            - number: 2.3
            - text: SUPPLY AIR DAMPER WILL MODULATE 100% OPEN WHEN OCCUPANCY IS DETECTED FOR MORE THAN 5 MINUTES (ADJ.).
            - assertion ids:
              - vav-occupied-open
          - Record:
            - number: 2.4
            - text: SUPPLY AIR WILL BE DELIVERED BASED ON DOWNSTREAM STATIC PRESSURE.
            - assertion ids:
              - vav-static
          - Record:
            - number: 3.1
            - text: VAV FAILURE: AN ALARM SHALL BE GENERATED WHEN THERE IS A DISCREPANCY BETWEEN DAMPER POSITION AND OCCUPANCY.
            - assertion ids:
              - vav-failure

  - controls context m512:
    - document id: 12__vol2__028
    - module role: M-512 written DOAS-1-and-2 controls context. This source sequence is recorded separately from M-511 DOAS-3; their different unoccupied behavior is not normalized into a single rule.
    - source sheet: M-512
    - source page: 8
    - network architecture:
      - protocols as printed:
        - BACNET
      - protocol context: The sequence calls for a factory-furnished onboard BACNET controller to communicate with the BAS. The BAS enables the system, and the printed I/O list is to be monitored and controlled from the GUI.
      - transport:
        - status: not_stated_in_supplied_excerpt
        - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
      - addressing baud rate controller model:
        - status: not_stated_in_supplied_excerpt
        - value: null (see recorded status/reason)
      - assertion ids:
        - doas12-factory-controller
        - doas12-bas-gui
    - sequences:
      - Record:
        - id: doas12
        - title: DEDICATED OUTSIDE AIR SYSTEM CONTROL SEQUENCE
        - equipment scope: DOAS 1&2 P&ID template; the supplied excerpt states no installed count, controller address, or individual device identity.
        - statements:
          - Record:
            - number: 1.1
            - text: THE DOAS SHALL BE A PACKAGED UNIT WITH A SUPPLY FAN AND EXHAUST FAN.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas12-packaged-fans
          - Record:
            - number: 1.2
            - text: THE DOAS SHALL BE PROVIDED WITH A VFD FOR THE SUPPLY FAN, AND A VFD FOR THE EXHAUST FAN.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas12-vfds
          - Record:
            - number: 1.3
            - text: THE DOAS SHALL BE CONTROLLED BY THE BAS AND REMAIN ENABLED AT ALL TIMES.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas12-bas-enabled
          - Record:
            - number: 1.3.1
            - text: DURING OCCUPIED HOURS, THE DOAS SHALL RUN CONTINUOUSLY AND DELIVER A CONSTANT AIR VOLUME CALCULATED PER ASHRAE 62.1.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas12-occupied
          - Record:
            - number: 1.3.2
            - text: DURING UNOCCUPIED HOURS, THE DOAS SHALL NOT RUN UNLESS A ZONE OVERRIDE COMMAND IS RECEIVED.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas12-unoccupied
          - Record:
            - number: 1.3.3
            - text: OCCUPIED/UNOCCUPIED HOURS SHALL BE DETERMINED BY BUILDING SCHEDULE IN THE BAS.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas12-schedule
          - Record:
            - number: 2
            - text: DOAS CONTROL: THE DOAS SYSTEM SHALL BE PROVIDED WITH A FACTORY FURNISHED ON-BOARD BACNET CONTROLLER THE MANUFACTURER SHALL USE ITS PREFERRED SEQUENCE TO MEET THE FOLLOWING REQUIREMENTS:
            - assertion ids:
              - doas12-factory-controller
          - Record:
            - number: 2.1
            - text: IN COOLING, THE CONTROLLER SHALL MONITOR THE CHILLED WATER COIL LEAVING AIR TEMPERATURE AND MODULATE CHILLED WATER FLOW TO MAINTAIN THE CHILLED WATER COIL LEAVING AIR TEMPERATURE SET POINT OF 55°F.
            - assertion ids:

          - Record:

            - text: IN HEATING, THE CONTROLLER SHALL MONITOR THE SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE AND MODULATE THE HOT WATER COIL TO MAINTAIN THE SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE SETPOINT OF 70°F.
            - assertion ids:
              - doas12-heating
          - Record:
            - number: 2.3
            - text: IN ECONOMIZER, THE UNIT SHALL RUN USING AN OUTSIDE AIR DRY-BULB BASED CONTROL STRATEGY.
            - assertion ids:

          - Record:
            - number: 3.1
            - text: SUPPLY AIR FAN: THE CONTROLLER SHALL MONITOR DUCT STATIC PRESSURE AND MODULATE SUPPLY AIR FLOW THROUGH THE VFD TO MAINTAIN DOWNSTREAM STATIC PRESSURE SET POINT. SENSOR LOCATION AND SETPOINT SHALL BE DETERMINED BY THE TAB FIRM.
            - assertion ids:

          - Record:
            - number: 3.2

          - Record:

ALL CONTROL POINTS ON THE I/O POINTS LIST SHALL BE MONITORED AND CONTROLLED FROM THE GRAPHIC USER INTERFACE (GUI).

## Equipment Reconciliation

Schedule-row identity and limitation reconciliation for the supplied nine-sheet archive excerpt. M-601 has 32 explicitly marked equipment schedule rows; M-505 adds 16 duct-silencer-design rows and 17 external-static-pressure rows. The missing plan sheets prevent conversion of any of these schedule/P&ID labels to a complete installed-equipment or physical-device count.

- schedule registry schema:

  - identity columns:

- schedule identity policy: Every M-601 marked row is retained as a source schedule-row identity. A source schedule row is not an installed-unit total. M-505 duct-silencer rows are quantity/location design selections rather than individually tagged equipment. M-505 external-static-pressure rows are performance-reference rows, and overlapping FCU/DOAS marks are not silently deduplicated or expanded without the missing plans.

## Supporting Evidence

Completion and limitation ledger for the supplied nine-sheet archive excerpt. It records what the visual review and authored modules establish, and what the absent remainder of the 191-sheet design set cannot establish.

- status: corroborated_document_component
- source limitations:
  - Record:
    - id: archive-excerpt-only
    - finding: The supplied PDF contains nine mechanical sheets M-505 through M-512 and M-601 from an original 191-sheet project. This record is complete only for these nine supplied pages.
  - Record:
    - id: no-mechanical-plans
    - finding: No mechanical floor/roof plan is supplied. Schedule rows, P&ID labels, templates, sensor roles and written sequences are not expanded into an installed equipment, sensor, actuator, controller, terminal, wire, or zone count.
  - Record:
    - id: no-separate-valve-or-actuator-schedules
    - finding: The reviewed excerpt contains no separately printed control-valve schedule or damper-actuator schedule. P&ID/sequence valve and bypass references remain in their own source contexts rather than becoming schedule rows.
  - Record:
    - id: p507-p508-sequence-scope
    - finding: M-507 and M-508 provide P&IDs and point matrices but no written sequence-of-operations block; M-509 provides the written hydronic sequences and is not retroactively treated as printed on the adjacent P&ID sheets.
  - Record:
    - id: physical-counts
    - finding: Repeated template matrix rows, source schedule-row marks, and P&ID local numeric tags are distinct units of evidence. They do not prove a physical quantity without missing plan/controller evidence.
- visual relationship scope: Every supplied source page was rendered and visually reviewed. Visual review confirms page/table/diagram disposition and page-level absence limits; dual-engine text corroboration proves the bounded authored table cells and text assertions. Neither mechanism is described as an automatic engineering interpretation.
- schedule note scope: All exact printed schedule cells, including notes fields and blank cells, are preserved in the M-505/M-601 source modules embedded by the schedules composite.
- inventory checks:
  - Record:
    - id: source-limitations
    - records path:
      - source_limitations
    - expected: 5
    - unique key: id

## Verification

MuPDF 1.27.2.2 and Poppler 26.03.0 independently captured all nine supplied pages. Current authored schedule/point cells and bounded text assertions are rechecked in both engines; composite modules do not infer any new PDF fact. Text agreement is not automatic engineering interpretation, physical symbol counting or proof of the missing 182 project sheets. Full-page Poppler renders were separately reviewed for every supplied page.

Re-run: `python3 ground_truth/tools/bundle_record.py verify ground_truth/records/12__vol2__028.json` from the collection directory.
